1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Training-Free Activation Sparsity.}
4	TEAL~\cite{liu2025teal} applies global top-$k$ selection by activation magnitude and achieves 40--50\% sparsity with no training cost.
5	CATS~\cite{lee2024cats} adapts per-layer thresholds for SiLU/SwiGLU activations; R-Sparse~\cite{zhang2025rsparse} predicts sparsity from rank-aware SVD projections; DIP~\cite{federici2025dip} combines dynamic input pruning with cache-aware masking; SparseInfer~\cite{shin2025sparseinfer} predicts activation sparsity patterns from attention outputs without additional training.
6	Wang et al.~\cite{wang2025dynamicsparse} address the interaction between dynamic sparsity and quantization with zigzag-patterned weight layouts.
7	OWL~\cite{yin2024owl} determines non-uniform per-layer sparsity ratios based on activation outlier distribution for weight pruning.
8	All training-free methods determine masks from fixed statistics or input-independent criteria, while our predictor generates \emph{input-dependent} masks trained to minimize output-distribution divergence.
9	
10	\paragraph{Predictor-Based Contextual Sparsity.}
11	DejaVu~\cite{liu2023dejavu} introduced learned sparsity predictors trained via BCE to classify each neuron independently.
12	ShadowLLM~\cite{akhauri2024shadowllm} uses attention-based routing with per-layer MSE supervision for intermediate coupling within each layer but without end-to-end gradient signal.
13	FastForward~\cite{gautam2026fastforward} adds a 67.2M-parameter compensation network using BCE for mask prediction, adding inference latency.
14	SVD-CSP~\cite{serbin2026svdcsp} compresses sparsity predictors via SVD decomposition for reduced predictor overhead.
15	PowerInfer~\cite{song2024powerinfer} exploits power-law activation frequencies for GPU--CPU scheduling.
16	MaskLLM~\cite{fang2024maskllm} shares the principle of frozen weights + learnable masks + KL training, but the two methods differ in three fundamental ways.

1	\section*{Ethics Statement}
2	
3	This work proposes a training method for sparsity predictors in pretrained language models, evaluated on publicly available models (LLaMA-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Qwen-2.5-14B~\cite{qwen2024qwen25}) and standard benchmarks (WikiText-2, WikiText-103, C4, ARC, WinoGrande, HellaSwag, PIQA, MMLU, GSM8K).
4	No new data collection, annotation, or human subjects are involved.
5	The method does not alter model outputs beyond the sparsity-induced degradation we analyze; it introduces no new generation capabilities or safety risks beyond those inherent in the base models.
6	Compute requirements are modest (1000 training steps, $\sim$45 minutes on a single GPU for KL training), presenting minimal environmental concern relative to the pretrained models we evaluate on.
7	We release no model weights or user-facing systems; our contribution is analytical (understanding KL distillation for sparsity) rather than deployment-oriented.
8	
9	\section*{Responsible NLP Research Checklist}
10	
11	\paragraph{A. Limitations and Risks.}
12	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
13	\item[\textbf{A1.}] Limitations are discussed in the Limitations section. Key limitations: evaluation limited to 7--14B models; perplexity is an imperfect proxy for downstream quality; wall-clock speedup requires sparse kernel support not implemented here.
14	\item[\textbf{A2.}] No foreseeable risks specific to this work beyond those inherent in the base language models. The method does not enable new capabilities or applications.
15	\end{itemize}
16	
17	\paragraph{B. Scientific Artifacts.}
18	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
19	\item[\textbf{B1.}] Yes. All models (LLaMA-3.1-8B~\cite{grattafiori2024llama3}, Mistral-7B-v0.3~\cite{jiang2023mistral}, Qwen-2.5-14B~\cite{qwen2024qwen25}), datasets (WikiText~\cite{merity2017pointer}, C4~\cite{raffel2020c4}), and methods (TEAL~\cite{liu2025teal}, SPON~\cite{xu2026spon}) are cited with original references.

×2, §4.4 ×2, §4.5, Conclusion），过度重复。\n- Table 3 caption 脚注 \"s42 oracle is from an earlier training run\" 是重要的方法论说明，不应仅以脚注形式出现。这解释了 s42 在 30% 时 gap 明显大于 s123/s456（PPL Δ=0.65 vs ≤0.04），建议在正文中讨论。\n- **为什么不评估 MSE intermediate objective？** 论文 taxonomy 明确包含该 cell。即使是简单的 per-layer hidden state MSE loss（训练成本与 BCE 相当）也能回答 coupling 程度的关键问题。这是有意识的 scope 限制还是实验资源不足？\n\n2. **C1 oracle masks 对 checkpoint 的敏感性？** C1 使用 KL predictor 最终 checkpoint 的 masks。如果在 step 500 提取 masks，BCE 是否仍能 match KL quality？这关系到 mask pattern 的发现是否需要完整训练。\n\n3. **14B 的 sparsity mismatch 影响？** KL 实际 29.6% vs TEAL 26.8%（Table 14）。如果控制 actual sparsity 相同，MMLU/GSM8K 差距会缩小多少？\n\n4. **Final-step SNR ratio？** Appendix G 仅报告到 step 300。Step 1000 时 KL vs BCE 的 SNR ratio 是多少？这对 \"coupling advantage\" narrative 至关重要。\n\n5. **单 predictor 多 sparsity 目标？** Limitations 提到这是 future work，但有没有初步实验？每个 sparsity target 训练一个 predictor 的成本 (~45 min × N 个 target) 在实际部署中不可忽视。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References / Comparisons\n\n- **MSE intermediate baseline**: ShadowLLM (Akhauri et al., 2024) 在 Table 1 中被归类为 Intermediate coupling，但未做实验对比。论文引用了 ShadowLLM 但仅在 related work 中讨论，未 experimentally compare。\n- **MaskLLM (Fang et al., 2024)**: Related work（§2, line 16–19）详细讨论了与 MaskLLM 的区别（N:M vs activation sparsity），但没有 ablation 验证这些区别是否 matters。虽然 sparsity type 不同，但 MaskLLM 也用 KL + Gumbel-Softmax training on frozen weights——at least citing their KL training results as reference point would contextualize the contribution.\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **C1 Target-Swap Ablation 设计精巧**（§4.4, Table 3）：这是全文最有说服力的实验。通过将 KL-discovered masks 转移到 BCE 训练，干净地分离了 \"mask quality\" 和 \"gradient coupling\" 的贡献。50% sparsity 下 max Δ≤0.79pp 的 match 非常有力。多 seed（s42/s123/s456）的交叉验证增强了可信度。\n\n2. **系统性消融覆盖全面**（§4.5, Table 4）：KL steps）表明结果对超参不敏感。\n\n3. **诚实报告负面结果**：14B 的 GSM8K −25pp 和 MMLU 逆转、50% sparsity 下 downstream collapse、无实际加速——这些在 Limitations 和正文中都有明确讨论。这是值得肯定的学术诚信。\n\n4. **Taxonomy 框架（Table 1）有分析价值**：Correction granularity × Training signal coupling 的二维分类清晰有效，为后续工作提供了有用的 conceptual map。尽管 intermediate cell 未被评估，框架本身是好的。\n\n5. **训练成本极低**：1000 步 / 45 分钟 / 单 GPU。Predictor <1% 基座参数。这使得方法高度可复现，降低了实验验证的门槛。\n\n6. **Must-Fix:**

**N1** [critical] 方法新颖性不足：核心贡献是 standard KL + standard Gumbel 的组合
- 核心方法是将 distillation 中标准的 forward KL divergence 与标准的 Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation 应用于 sparsity predictor 训练。MaskLLM (Fang et al., 2024) 已在 NeurIPS 2024 证明了 "frozen weights + learnable masks + KL training" 的有效性。虽然论文指出了与 MaskLLM 的三点区别（activation vs weight sparsity, predictor vs learnable params, global top-k vs N:M），但核心 principle（用 KL 训练 mask）是相同的。Loss function swap 本身（BCE→KL）在 distillation 领域是 well-known 的选择，缺乏足够的方法论贡献来支撑一篇 long paper。

**N2** [critical] 下游任务结果与 PPL 改善严重不匹配，削弱实用价值
- 30% sparsity on LLaMA-8B：KL uniform MMLU 40.7±1.3% vs TEAL 45.86%（-5.2pp），GSM8K 7.5±0.1% vs TEAL 8.79%（-1.3pp）。KL 在 PPL 上赢但在 MMLU/GSM8K 上输。
- Mistral-7B：GSM8K 11.1% vs TEAL 15.9%（-4.8pp）
- Qwen-14B：GSM8K -25pp, MMLU -2.79pp（uniform），更大 scale 上劣势加剧
- 50% sparsity：所有方法 MMLU 坍塌至近随机
- 如果 PPL 改善不能转化为下游任务改善，那么 "output-distribution distillation" 的实用意义需要重新审视。

**Should-Fix:**

**W1** Taxonomy（Table 1）的 Intermediate (MSE) 列缺少实验支撑
**W2** 无实际 wall-clock speedup 验证
**W3** Conclusion 过于简短（3 句话 + vspace{-14pt}）
**W4** Baseline 不足：仅与 vanilla TEAL 对比，缺少 CATS/R-Sparse/ShadowLLM
**W5** C1 ablation at 30% s42 seed 的 PPL gap 较大（0.65 PPL），oracle 版本敏感

**Minor:**
M1: Table 2 中 "Ours" 标注在 global 和 uniform 两行出现，推荐配置不统一
M2: SNR 仅 step 300，建议补充纵向曲线
M3: Figure 2 caption "disproportionately" overclaiming
M4: Abstract 对 14B 结果过度简化（未区分 uniform vs global）
M5: Griffin reference not_found; AdamW year mismatch

---

请对以上每一条给出 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT] 回应。

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 EMNLP 2026 圆桌审稿 Reviewer 1。请对以下论文进行独立初审。

## 论文信息
- 标题: Output-Distribution Distillation for Contextual Sparsity Prediction in SwiGLU LLMs
- 路径: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.tex
- PDF: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.pdf
- 目标 venue: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026)
- 截止日期: 2026-05-25

## Venue 要求
- Long paper: 正文最多 8 页，参考文献不限页数
- Limitations section 必须有，不计入页数
- Ethics statement 可选，不计入页数
- ACL 模板 (review 模式)，double-blind
- 附录允许，但审稿人不必阅读
- Overall Assessment 评分: 5=Consider for Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重：方法严谨性 + 实验验证

请重点审查以下方面：

1. **方法严谨性**：
   - KL distillation 公式推导是否正确
   - 梯度分析（§3.6 BCE vs KL gradient）逻辑是否自洽
   - Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation 和 adaptive penalty controller 设计是否合理
   - Taxonomy（Table 1: Decomposed/Intermediate/Coupled）分类是否准确

2. **实验验证**：
   - 主实验（Table 2）数值是否内部一致（正文引用 vs 表格数据）
   - C1 target-swap ablation（Table 3）设计是否能支撑 "mask quality drives inference-stage quality" 的 claim
   - 消融实验（Table 4）覆盖是否充分
   - 多 seed 统计是否充分（哪些有 3-seed，哪些只有单 seed）
   - 跨架构评估（Mistral, Qwen-14B）统计强度
   - 数值精度：正文中的百分比计算是否准确（如 "19% reduction", "50% reduction", "7.3× smaller σ"）
   - MSE baseline 结果（PPL 20.43 at 30%, 229.26 at 50%）是否合理，是否存在实现缺陷

3. **格式合规**：
   - 正文是否在 8 页以内
   - Limitations section 是否存在且充分

请按以下格式输出审稿意见：
- Summary（2-3句）
- Strengths（逐条）
- Weaknesses（逐条，标注 [Must-Fix] / [Should-Fix] / [Minor]）
- Questions（需要作者澄清的）
- Missing References（如有）
- Overall Assessment（评分 + 置信度）
- Detailed Comments（逐节）

## Reviewer Confidence
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully. It's unlikely, though conceivable, that I missed something that should affect my ratings."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something. Although I have a good feel for this area in general, I did not carefully check the paper's details, e.g., the math or experimental design."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details, didn't understand some central points, or can't be sure about the novelty of the work."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand. My evaluation is just an educated guess."

## Soundness
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims. Some extra experiments could be nice, but not essential."
- **3**: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims. Some minor points may need extra support or details."
- **2**: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported. There are major technical/methodological problems."
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication or is not relevant to ACL."
- *Intermediate options available: 4.5, 3.5, 2.5, 1.5*

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
5	
6	\paragraph{Model and Predictor.}
7	We evaluate on LLaMA-3.1-8B~\cite{grattafiori2024llama3}, which uses SwiGLU FFN blocks~\cite{shazeer2020glu} with $d_{\text{model}}=4096$ and $d_{\text{ff}}=14336$ across 32 layers.
8	The sparsity predictor is a two-layer MLP with 2.37M parameters per layer (75.9M total), implemented as Linear($4096 \to 128$) + GELU + Linear($128 \to 14336$), trained to produce per-neuron importance scores that minimize output-distribution divergence under sparsity.
9	
10	\paragraph{Training.}
11	All predictors are trained for 1000 steps on WikiText-103 with sequence length 2048 and batch size 4 on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPU (96\,GB), using AdamW~\cite{loshchilov2019adamw} with learning rate $1 \times 10^{-3}$, weight decay $5 \times 10^{-2}$, and cosine decay.
12	The KL predictor uses Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation~\cite{jang2017gumbel} with temperature $\tau=1.0$ annealed to $0.1$ over training.
13	
14	\paragraph{Evaluation.}
15	We report perplexity on WikiText-2~\cite{merity2017pointer} (seq\_len=2048) and downstream accuracy on ARC-Challenge (25-shot), WinoGrande (5-shot), MMLU (5-shot), GSM8K (8-shot), HellaSwag (10-shot), and PIQA (0-shot).
16	
17	\paragraph{Inference Protocol.}
18	At inference, the predictor produces per-neuron scores that are passed to TEAL's global top-$k$ allocation mechanism~\cite{liu2025teal}, which distributes the sparsity budget across layers non-uniformly.
19	We term this \textit{predictor-TEAL}: the predictor replaces TEAL's magnitude-based scoring while retaining its allocation strategy.
20	
21	\paragraph{Baselines.}
22	We compare against: (1) \textbf{Vanilla TEAL}~\cite{liu2025teal}, which uses activation magnitudes directly\footnote{TEAL is deterministic; single-run evaluation suffices. KL/BCE predictors require multi-seed evaluation due to random initialization.}; (2) \textbf{BCE predictor} (Deja
…[truncated 17374 chars]

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	We present a KL distillation framework for training contextual sparsity predictors in SwiGLU-based language models (Figure~\ref{fig:overview}).
5	The method replaces per-neuron binary classification (BCE) with an output-distribution objective that couples all pruning decisions through the model's forward pass and achieves competitive performance without auxiliary error compensation networks at inference.
6	
7	\subsection{Problem Formulation}
8	\label{sec:method:formulation}
9	
10	Consider a pretrained transformer with $L$ layers, each containing a SwiGLU feed-forward network~\cite{shazeer2020glu}.
11	For layer $\ell$, the FFN computes:
12	\begin{align}
13	\mathbf{y}^{(\ell)} = \bigl(\sigma(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}) \odot \mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)}\bigr) \mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)},
14	\label{eq:swiglu}
15	\end{align}
16	where $\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d}$ is the hidden state after attention ($d{=}4096$), $\sigma$ denotes SiLU, $\mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}, \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d_{\text{ff}}}$ and $\mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{\text{ff}} \times d}$ are the gate, up-projection, and down-projection matrices, and $d_{\text{ff}}{=}14{,}336$.
17	
18	Define the gate activation $\mathbf{g}^{(\ell)} = \sigma(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}) \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{\text{ff}}}$.
19	Each element $g_j^{(\ell)}$ determines the contribution of neuron $j$ to the output.
20	We introduce a binary mask $\mathbf{m}^{(\ell)} \in \{0,1\}^{d_{\text{ff}}}$ that zeros out pruned neurons:
21	\begin{align}
22	\mathbf{y}_{\text{sparse}}^{(\ell)} = \bigl(\mathbf{m}^{(\ell)} \odot \mathbf{g}^{(\ell)} \odot \mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)}\bigr) \mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)}.
23	\label{eq:sparse_ffn}
24	\end{align}
25	Our goal is to learn a mask predictor $f_\theta^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)}) \to \mathbf{s}^{(\ell)} \in (0,1)^{d_{\text{ff}}}$ that produces per-neuron importan
…[truncated 11308 chars]

_source line 809 · 2026-05-20T05:59:30.122219+00:00_

1	Learned sparsity predictors for SwiGLU (Swish-Gated Linear Unit) LLMs universally use binary cross-entropy (BCE), training each neuron independently, yet inference allocates budgets globally via top-$k$ ranking.
2	We train predictors with Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, coupling all mask decisions through the output distribution.
3	At 30\% sparsity on LLaMA-3.1-8B, the KL predictor ($<$1\% of base parameters) reduces perplexity by 19\% relative to TEAL with moderate downstream degradation, and renders post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) redundant in prediction quality.
4	KL-derived masks diverge 46--68\% from magnitude-based masks (depending on sparsity level); a target-swap ablation confirms that these mask targets, discovered through KL's coupled gradients, are the proximate quality driver when transferred to simpler objectives.
5	Per-layer coupling (MSE) degrades below training-free baselines ($1.9\times$ TEAL at 30\%; $9.6\times$ at 50\%), revealing \emph{coupling alignment}---not coupling per se---as decisive.
6	Preliminary single-seed evaluation on Mistral-7B-v0.3 and cross-scale evaluation on Qwen-2.5-14B (uniform allocation) reveal consistent PPL improvements but scale-dependent downstream effects.
7	At 50\% sparsity the perplexity advantage widens, though downstream reasoning collapses to near-random regardless of method, revealing a capacity cliff.
8

_source line 811 · 2026-05-20T05:59:30.625668+00:00_

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	SwiGLU feed-forward networks~\cite{shazeer2020glu} in modern LLMs exhibit high contextual sparsity: at any given token, a large fraction of FFN neurons contribute negligibly to the output.
4	SwiGLU's gated activation makes prediction challenging: importance depends on both gate and up-projection values, a target that magnitude-based methods handle suboptimally.
5	Learned sparsity predictors~\cite{liu2023dejavu,gautam2026fastforward} exploit this property by training small networks to anticipate which neurons are important, but they universally rely on binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, treating each neuron as an independent classification target.
6	Yet at inference, sparsity budgets are allocated globally: the TEAL (training-free activation sparsity;~\citealp{liu2025teal}) top-$k$ mechanism ranks all neurons across all layers and retains only those above a model-wide threshold.
7	This mismatch between per-neuron training and global inference allocation has not been formally analyzed.
8	
9	Gradient analysis reveals a structural asymmetry: BCE's gradient $\partial \mathcal{L}_{\text{BCE}} / \partial s_j = \sigma(s_j) - m_j^*$ depends only on neuron $j$, while under KL divergence, the gradient couples each neuron to all others through the downstream Jacobian (\S\ref{sec:method:gradient}).
10	KL's coupled gradients discover neuron importance patterns that diverge 68\% from magnitude-based masks (per-layer Jaccard 32\%).
11	A target-swap ablation confirms that these discovered mask targets are the proximate quality driver at inference: BCE trained on KL-derived targets reproduces KL quality (max $\Delta$$\leq$0.79pp at 50\%, s42), while magnitude-trained BCE achieves 28.13$\pm$0.20. Gradient coupling remains necessary at the discovery stage.
12	
13	We instantiate a lightweight predictor (75.9M params, $<$1\% of base) trained with forward KL and Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation~\cite{jang2017gumbel} on LLaMA-3.1-8B~\cite{grattafiori2024llama3}.
14	At 
…[truncated 1876 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	\vspace{-18pt}
4	
5	A target-swap ablation confirms that KL-discovered mask patterns (46--68\% divergent from magnitude) drive inference-stage quality.
6	A predictor ($<$1\% of base) reduces TEAL's perplexity by 19\% at 30\%, renders compensation redundant, and delivers $7.3\times$ smaller cross-seed $\sigma$ under uniform allocation.
7	Cross-architecture evaluation confirms the pattern (Mistral-7B: 5/6 tasks); at 14B, reasoning declines despite PPL gains.
8	Future work: MSE intermediate coupling, mask divergence analysis, and N:M sparsity.
9

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our primary evaluation uses WikiText-2 perplexity, and downstream benchmarks reveal that perplexity is a poor proxy for reasoning ability at moderate sparsity: 40\% sparsity achieves PPL 8.36 but MMLU already degrades; 30\% appears to be the lowest-degradation sparsity level for downstream fidelity.
4	Training requires 1000 steps on WikiText-103 ($\sim$45 minutes on RTX PRO 6000 for KL, $\sim$17 minutes for BCE), but a separate predictor must be trained for each sparsity target.
5	On Mistral-7B, KL wins 5 of 6 downstream tasks at 30\% but GSM8K reverses (11.1\% vs.\ TEAL's 15.9\%), consistent with GSM8K's sensitivity to the sparsity objective: reasoning tasks may require different optimization targets than knowledge and commonsense benchmarks; forward KL's mass-covering property may preserve output breadth at the expense of reasoning-critical pathways.
6	The 30\% sparsity analysis is validated at 7--8B scale; at 14B, KL's MMLU decline ($-$2.79pp uniform, $-$8.1pp global) and GSM8K reversal ($-$20pp uniform; 32.1$\pm$5.2\% vs.\ TEAL's 52.1\%, 3-seed) reflect task-and-scale-dependent effects (\S\ref{sec:cross_arch}; Appendix~\ref{app:14b}): at 8B all methods collapse to $<$9\% GSM8K, so this reversal emerges only with sufficient base capacity. KL's high cross-seed GSM8K variance ($\sigma$=5.2pp) suggests minor training perturbations disrupt reasoning-critical pathways that magnitude pruning preserves more robustly. Scaling to 70B remains untested.
7	Out-of-distribution evaluation on C4 (Appendix~\ref{app:ood}) shows the KL advantage transfers but with a wider gap: C4 PPL at 30\% is $1.74\times$ dense vs.\ $1.46\times$ on in-domain WikiText-2, which indicates that the predictor partially overfits to training-domain activation patterns. Extending OOD evaluation to 70\% sparsity is left to future work.
8	The target-swap ablation (\S\ref{sec:mask_analysis}) confirms that mask quality drives inference-stage quality, with KL's coupled gradients
…[truncated 1311 chars]

_source line 817 · 2026-05-20T05:59:36.668426+00:00_

1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Training-Free Activation Sparsity.}
4	TEAL~\cite{liu2025teal} applies global top-$k$ selection by activation magnitude and achieves 40--50\% sparsity with no training cost.
5	CATS~\cite{lee2024cats} adapts per-layer thresholds for SiLU/SwiGLU activations; R-Sparse~\cite{zhang2025rsparse} predicts sparsity from rank-aware SVD projections; DIP~\cite{federici2025dip} combines dynamic input pruning with cache-aware masking; SparseInfer~\cite{shin2025sparseinfer} predicts activation sparsity patterns from attention outputs without additional training.
6	Wang et al.~\cite{wang2025dynamicsparse} address the interaction between dynamic sparsity and quantization with zigzag-patterned weight layouts.
7	OWL~\cite{yin2024owl} determines non-uniform per-layer sparsity ratios based on activation outlier distribution for weight pruning.
8	All training-free methods determine masks from fixed statistics or input-independent criteria, while our predictor generates \emph{input-dependent} masks trained to minimize output-distribution divergence.
9	
10	\paragraph{Predictor-Based Contextual Sparsity.}
11	DejaVu~\cite{liu2023dejavu} introduced learned sparsity predictors trained via BCE to classify each neuron independently.
12	ShadowLLM~\cite{akhauri2024shadowllm} uses attention-based routing with per-layer MSE supervision for intermediate coupling within each layer but without end-to-end gradient signal.
13	FastForward~\cite{gautam2026fastforward} adds a 67.2M-parameter compensation network using BCE for mask prediction, adding inference latency.
14	SVD-CSP~\cite{serbin2026svdcsp} compresses sparsity predictors via SVD decomposition for reduced predictor overhead.
15	PowerInfer~\cite{song2024powerinfer} exploits power-law activation frequencies for GPU--CPU scheduling.
16	MaskLLM~\cite{fang2024maskllm} shares the principle of frozen weights + learnable masks + KL training, but the two methods differ in three fundamental ways.
17	First, \emph{spar

3	\section{Inference Efficiency}
4	\label{app:latency}
5	
6	Table~\ref{tab:latency} reports inference throughput on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPU (96\,GB; batch size 1, sequence length 2048, fp16, averaged over 100 forward passes).
7	
8	\begin{table}[h]
9	\centering
10	\caption{Inference latency on LLaMA-3.1-8B (RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, batch=1, seq=2048, fp16, 100 forward passes). Overhead is relative to dense inference.}
11	\label{tab:latency}
12	\small
13	\begin{tabular}{llcccc}
14	\toprule
15	Method & Sparsity & ms/fwd & tok/s & Overhead \\
16	\midrule
17	Dense & 0\% & 119.11$\pm$0.57 & 17{,}195 & --- \\
18	\midrule
19	KL predictor-only & 30\% & 126.64$\pm$0.70 & 16{,}172 & +6.3\% \\ % exp_id: latency_bench
20	KL predictor-only & 50\% & 126.78$\pm$0.31 & 16{,}154 & +6.4\% \\ % exp_id: latency_bench
21	\midrule
22	Predictor + compensation & 30\% & 129.94$\pm$0.75 & 15{,}761 & +9.1\% \\ % exp_id: latency_bench
23	Predictor + compensation & 50\% & 129.71$\pm$0.53 & 15{,}789 & +8.9\% \\ % exp_id: latency_bench
24	\bottomrule
25	\end{tabular}
26	\end{table}
27	
28	The full KL-sparse pipeline adds 6.4\% overhead to dense inference (126.78 vs.\ 119.11\,ms/forward), of which the predictor's 32-layer forward pass contributes 3.17\,ms (2.7\%); the remainder arises from mask application and sparse FFN indexing.
29	Eliminating the compensation network saves an additional $\sim$2.7\% latency overhead and 67.2M parameters (the per-layer GEMV required for output rescaling); KL distillation thus provides both quality and efficiency advantages over BCE-based approaches.
30	The overhead is near-identical at 30\% and 50\% sparsity because the predictor runs the same computation regardless of target sparsity; only the top-$k$ threshold changes.
31	
32	Note that the current implementation uses unstructured element-wise masks, which do not translate to wall-clock speedup without sparse kernel support.

1	\section*{Ethics Statement}
2	
3	This work proposes a training method for sparsity predictors in pretrained language models, evaluated on publicly available models (LLaMA-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Qwen-2.5-14B~\cite{qwen2024qwen25}) and standard benchmarks (WikiText-2, WikiText-103, C4, ARC, WinoGrande, HellaSwag, PIQA, MMLU, GSM8K).
4	No new data collection, annotation, or human subjects are involved.
5	The method does not alter model outputs beyond the sparsity-induced degradation we analyze; it introduces no new generation capabilities or safety risks beyond those inherent in the base models.
6	Compute requirements are modest (1000 training steps, $\sim$45 minutes on a single GPU for KL training), presenting minimal environmental concern relative to the pretrained models we evaluate on.
7	We release no model weights or user-facing systems; our contribution is analytical (understanding KL distillation for sparsity) rather than deployment-oriented.
8	
9	\section*{Responsible NLP Research Checklist}
10

12	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
13	\item[\textbf{A1.}] Limitations are discussed in the Limitations section. Key limitations: evaluation limited to 7--14B models; perplexity is an imperfect proxy for downstream quality; wall-clock speedup requires sparse kernel support not implemented here.
14	\item[\textbf{A2.}] No foreseeable risks specific to this work beyond those inherent in the base language models. The method does not enable new capabilities or applications.
15	\end{itemize}

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 2 的完整审稿意见。请逐条回应：对每个 Weakness/Question，标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 2 初审报告

### 总体评分
- **Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)
- **Soundness**: 3.5/5
- **Excitement**: 2.5/5
- **Reproducibility**: 4/5
- **Confidence**: 4/5

### Summary
本文研究在 SwiGLU LLM 的上下文稀疏预测器训练中用 KL 散度替代 BCE。KL 训练发现与 magnitude mask 偏离 46–68% 的 mask pattern，LLaMA-3.1-8B 30% sparsity 下 PPL 改善 19%。Target-swap 消融（C1）证明 mask 质量而非梯度耦合本身驱动推理质量。但 PPL 改善未转化为下游收益（TEAL 在 MMLU/GSM8K 上均优于 KL），无实际加速，跨架构结论不 robust。

### Must-Fix 问题

**W1. PPL 改善与下游任务脱节。** Table 2 显示 30% sparsity 下 TEAL uniform 在 MMLU (45.86 vs 40.7) 和 GSM8K (8.79 vs 7.5) 上均优于 KL uniform。Abstract 称 "moderate downstream degradation" 但 MMLU -37.6%, GSM8K -84.6% (relative to dense)。论文核心叙事 "KL discovers better masks" 需要重新界定——better for PPL, worse for downstream。

**W2. "Cross-architecture" claim 证据不足。** Mistral-7B 仅单 seed 无 std；Qwen-14B 下游严重退化（GSM8K -20pp, MMLU -2.79pp uniform）。Introduction 称 "cross-architecture evaluation confirms the pattern" 但 pattern 高度不一致。

**W3. 与 MaskLLM 的新颖性区分不充分。** MaskLLM (NeurIPS 2024) 已使用 frozen weights + learnable masks + KL + Gumbel。核心思想（KL 比 BCE 好）已在 weight sparsity 上发表。论文需解释 activation sparsity 场景下的增量贡献。

### Should-Fix 问题

**W4.** 无实际推理加速，predictor 增加 6.4% overhead。
**W5.** "Coupling alignment" 概念有循环论证嫌疑，缺乏形式化定义。
**W6.** Conclusion 仅 4 行 + vspace{-18pt}，不符合 long paper 规范。
**W7.** 训练数据极小（~8M tokens），C4 OOD gap 显著（1.74× vs 1.46× dense）。

### Missing References
1. ActTail (2026, arXiv:2603.12272)
2. "A Sparsity Predicting Approach via Activation Pattern Clustering" (2025)
3. "Universal Properties of Activation Sparsity in Modern LLMs" (2025)

---

请对以上每一条 W1-W7 和 Missing References 逐条 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]。

  "text": "## Reviewer 1 对 Reviewer 2 初审意见的逐条回应\n\n---\n\n### W1. PPL 改善与下游任务脱节\n\n[AGREE] R2-W1: 核心观察成立，PPL 改善确实未一致转化为下游增益。我在自己的审稿中也将此列为 N1 Must-Fix。\n\n但 R2 的 framing 有误导之处：R2 称 \"MMLU -37.6%, GSM8K -84.6%\"，这是**相对 dense 的退化**，不是 KL vs TEAL 的差距。实际 KL vs TEAL 的对比是：\n- MMLU: 40.7 vs 45.86（差 5.16pp，两者都已从 dense 65.27 大幅退化）\n- GSM8K: 7.5 vs 8.79（差仅 1.29pp，TEAL 自身也已从 dense 48.75 退化 82%）\n\nGSM8K 上两种方法都几乎崩溃，1.29pp 的差异在统计上不显著（KL 的 std=0.1）。MMLU 5.16pp 的差距是真实的、需要作者解释的问题。但 R2 将其表述为 \"GSM8K -84.6%\" 是对比基线选择错误。\n\n此外，KL 在 ARC-c（43.7 vs 39.33, +4.4pp）和 WG（68.9 vs 65.04, +3.9pp）上优于 TEAL（Table 2），以及 HellaSwag（+7.8pp）和 PIQA（+2.2pp）（Table 12, Appendix H）。总体画面是\"PPL 和 4/6 下游任务更好，MMLU 更差，GSM8K 持平\"，而非 R2 所述的全面\"worse for downstream\"。\n\n**结论**：同意 MMLU 退化需要正面解决，但 R2 的总结过于负面。\n\n---\n\n### W2. Cross-architecture claim 证据不足\n\n[AGREE] R2-W2: 同意。我在自己的审稿中也将此列为 N4 Should-Fix。Mistral 单 seed 无 std，Qwen-14B 全局分配也是单 seed。Introduction 中 \"cross-architecture evaluation confirms the pattern\" 的表述确实过强。不过 Abstract 中 \"Preliminary single-seed evaluation\" 的措辞是准确的（abstract.tex line 6）。建议 Introduction 也采用相同的审慎措辞。\n\n---\n\n### W3. 与 MaskLLM 的新颖性区分不充分\n\n[REBUT] R2-W3: R2 声称 \"核心思想（KL 比 BCE 好）已在 weight sparsity 上发表\"。但 MaskLLM 和本文解决的是**根本不同的问题**，论文已在 §2 Related Work（related_work.tex lines 16-19）明确区分了三个维度：\n\n1. **稀疏类型**：MaskLLM 优化 N:M 半结构化 **weight** sparsity（静态 2:4 pattern），本文优化动态 per-token **activation** sparsity。Weight mask 训练后固定；本文的 mask 随每个输入 token 变化。\n2. **Mask 生成机制**：MaskLLM 的 mask 是直接可学习参数（per weight group），通过 Gumbel-Softmax 选择。本文训练一个 predictor network，从 hidden state 推断 mask——这是一个**泛化问题**，predictor 必须对未见过的输入生成好的 mask。\n3. **分配机制**：MaskLLM 在固定 N:M 组内优化，无跨组竞争；本文使用全局 top-k 预算跨所有层分配。这种全局竞争正是 inter-neuron dependency 的来源，也是 coupling 有意义的根本原因。\n\n更关键的是，MaskLLM 的\"KL > BCE\"不能直接推广到 activation sparsity：MaskLLM 的 mask 参数是直接优化的（类似 fine-tuning），而 activation sparsity 需要训练一个必须**泛化到新输入**的 predictor。这两个场景对目标函数的要求完全不同。本文的贡献不是\"KL > BCE\"这个通用结论，而是在 dynamic prediction + global allocation 这个特定组合下的实证分析，包括 C1 ablation、taxonomy、coupling alignment 概念等。\n\n**结论**：MaskLLM 的区分在论文中已充分讨论。\"核心思想已发表\"的说法忽略了问题设置的根本差异。\n\n---\n\n### W4. 无实际推理加速\n\n[AGREE] R2-W4: 同意这是实际局限性。论文在 Introduction（line 18）和 Appendix A 中均明确说明了这一点。§1 明确说 \"Wall-clock speedup requires sparse kernel support not addressed here\"。6.4% overhead 来自 predictor 前向传播和 mask indexing，需要 Triton/cuSPARSE 支持才能实现净加速。\n\n但需要指出：(a) 论文定位为实证分析研究（\"we do not propose a new architecture or algorithm\"），而非系统加速论文；(b) KL predictor 的一个实际贡献是**消除 67.2M 参数的 compensation network**（§4.5, Appendix I），减少 2.7% latency overhead，这是面向部署的具体改进。\n\n**结论**：同意局限性，但论文的定位和透明度是合理的。\n\n---\n\n### W5. \"Coupling alignment\" 循环论证嫌疑\n\n[AGREE] R2-W5（部分同意）: R2 的批评有一定道理。\"Coupling alignment\" 的定义本质上是\"与最终输出分布对齐的耦合\"——这在逻辑上接近于\"use the right objective\"的同义反复。\n\n但论文确实提供了**非平凡的实验证据**来支撑这一概念：\n- MSE 提供 per-layer coupling 但 PPL 灾难性崩溃（229.26 at 50%，Table 4），说明\"有耦合\"不够\n- KL 的 SNR 低于 BCE（2.3×，Appendix G），说明\"梯度质量更好\"也不够\n- C1 ablation 显示 mask 质量可以从 KL 转移到 BCE（Table 3），说明耦合仅在 discovery 阶段必要\n\n这些结果合在一起为 \"alignment matters, not coupling per se\" 提供了实证支持。问题在于缺乏形式化定义——\"coupling alignment\"何时成立、何时不成立？除了 {BCE, MSE, KL} 之外还有什么预测？\n\n**结论**：同意概念需要更精确的定义，但不同意\"循环论证\"——实验证据提供了非平凡的区分。\n\n---\n\n### W6. Conclusion 过短 + vspace hack\n\n[AGREE] R2-W6: 完全同意。我在自己的审稿中也标记了此问题（W2 Minor）。Conclusion 仅 3 句 + `\\vspace{-18pt}` 是不合规的空间操纵。建议将 §4.6 部分内容移至 Appendix，给 Conclusion 留出合理空间。\n\n---\n\n### W7. 训练数据极小，OOD gap 显著\n\n[AGREE] R2-W7（部分同意）: 训练仅用 ~8M tokens（1000 steps × batch 4 × seq 2048）确实较少。C4 OOD gap（1.74× vs 1.46×）是 Limitations 中已报告的事实（limitations.tex line 7）。\n\n但需要考虑：\n- 低训练成本（~45 min 单 GPU）是方法的**实际优势**之一\n- Appendix D 的 C4 评估显示 KL 在 OOD 上仍**大幅优于 TEAL**（50% sparsity: C4 PPL 18.40 vs 38.75，Table 11）\n- 更多训练数据是否能缩小 OOD gap 是一个合理的实验建议，但不影响当前结论的有效性\n\n**结论**：同意 OOD gap 是需要 future work 解决的问题，但论文的透明报告已足够。增加训练数据的消融实验会加强论文。\n\n---\n\n### Missing References\n\n1. **ActTail (2026, arXiv:2603.12272)**: 需要验证此引用是否真实存在。如果存在且讨论 activation sparsity 的尾部分布特征，确实应该引用。\n2. **\"A Sparsity Predicting Approach via Activation Pattern Clustering\" (2025)**: 需要验证。标题与本文方向相关。\n3. **\"Universal Properties of Activation Sparsity in Modern LLMs\" (2025)**: 需要验证。如果存在，与 Sparsing Law (Luo et al., 2024) 的引用地位类似。\n\n**总评**：R2 提出的 3 篇引用需要先验证真实性。建议 Director 让作者核查。论文已有 47 篇引用，覆盖了该领域的主要工作。\n\n---\n\n### 对 R2 Overall Assessment 的评论\n\nR2 给出 Overall 3.0 (Findings)、Excitement 2.5。我认为 Excitement 2.5 偏低——C1 target-swap ablation 的实验设计不是标准操作，taxonomy 提供了有用的概念框架，gradient SNR 分析给出反直觉洞见。这些超越了\"直接的目标函数替换\"。\n\n但 R2 关于 W1（PPL-downstream 脱节）和 W3（与 MaskLLM 区分）的核心批评与我的审稿互相验证。如果作者能充分回应 PPL-downstream 矛盾（R1-N1 = R2-W1）和 MSE baseline 公平性（R1-N2），论文可提升至 3.5。当前 3.0-3.5 的区间是合理的。",

}

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 1，负责**方法严谨性 + 实验完整性**。

## Venue 信息
- **目标会议**：EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR May 2026 cycle）
- **截止日期**：2026-05-25
- **页数限制**：正文 8 页，参考文献不限，Limitations/Ethics 不计入页数
- **模板**：ACL style template（double-blind review）
- **评分标准**：5=Award, 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Reject

## 论文路径
`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.tex`

## 审查重点

### 1. 方法严谨性
- **梯度分析（§3.6）**：BCE vs KL 梯度结构对比是否数学上正确？"downstream Jacobian" coupling 的推导是否成立？SNR 分析（Appendix G）是否支撑 "target alignment not gradient clarity" 的结论？
- **Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation（§3.3）**：温度退火策略（1.0→0.1）的理论依据？与 STE 对比的公平性？
- **Adaptive penalty controller（§3.5）**：几何倍增策略的收敛保证？30% sparsity 时 λ 到 5000 cap 是否表明控制器设计不佳？
- **Coupling alignment 概念（§3.7）**：这个概念是否有可操作的定义？是否存在同义反复（"只有 output-level 目标才能发现好的 output-level mask"）？

### 2. 实验设计与统计
- **Baselines 公平性**：BCE 训了 2700 步 vs KL 1000 步——"matched wall-clock" 是否充分？MSE baseline 只有 single seed，合理吗？
- **Target-swap ablation（Table 3）**：C1 的 s42 oracle 来自不同训练 run，这是否引入 confound？retrained seeds 的 delta 范围（≤1.18pp）是否足够小？
- **数值一致性**：检查 Table 1/2/3/4 之间的数值是否一致（同一 exp_id 在不同表格中是否报告相同数值）。
- **3-seed 评估**：哪些实验有 3-seed，哪些只有 single seed？single-seed 结果的可靠性如何？
- **Cross-architecture（§4.6）**：Mistral 和 Qwen 只有 single seed，"preliminary" 标注是否充分？14B 上 GSM8K 下降 20pp 但 PPL 改善——这是否削弱了核心主张？

### 3. 关键数值审查
- PPL 19% 改善的 headline claim vs matched sparsity 14.5% 
- 50% sparsity 下 downstream collapse 到 near-random，这对论文核心价值有何影响？
- BCE+Gumbel (32.22) 比标准 BCE (27.97) 更差——解释是否令人信服？

请按 ACL ARR 标准输出完整审稿报告，包括：Summary, Strengths, Weaknesses (Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Minor), Questions, Missing References, Overall Assessment (1-5 分 + 置信度)。

# ACL ARR Review Form – Scoring Dimensions

## Overall Assessment
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5:** "Consider for Award: I think this paper could be considered for an outstanding paper award"
- **4:** Conference acceptance recommended
- **3:** "Findings: I think this paper could be accepted to the Findings of the ACL"
- **2:** Needs substantial revisions by next cycle
- **1:** Do not resubmit or not relevant to ACL

## Soundness
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5:** "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- **4:** "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims"
- **3:** "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims"
- **2:** "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported"
- **1:** "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## Excitement
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5:** "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others"
- **4:** "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others"
- **3:** "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others"
- **2:** "Potentially Interesting: does not resonate with me, but might with others"
- **1:** "Not Exciting: does not resonate with me, and I don't think it would with others"

## Reviewer Confidence
**Scale:** 1-5
- **5:** Positive evaluation correct; very careful reading
- **4:** Quite sure; unlikely to have missed critical points
- **3:** Pretty sure; some details may have been missed
- **2:** Willing to defend, but likely missed some details
- **1:** Not expert area or paper very hard to understand

## Reproducibility
**Scale:** 1-5
- **5:** "They could easily reproduce the results"
- **4:** "They could mostly reproduce the results"
- **3:** "They could reproduce the results with some difficulty"
- **2:** "They would be hard pressed to reproduce the results"
- **1:** "They would not be able to reproduce the results"

## Additional Dimensions

```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 1 完整审稿报告\n\n**论文**: Output-Distribution Distillation for Contextual Sparsity Prediction in SwiGLU LLMs\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle)\n**审查侧重**: 方法严谨性 + 实验完整性\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall Assessment**: 3.5 / 5 — Borderline Conference\n**Confidence**: 4 / 5 — Quite sure; careful reading of method and all appendices\n\n**一句话总结**: 有意义的实证发现（KL 蒸馏发现更优稀疏 mask）通过巧妙的 target-swap 消融得到支撑，但 coupling alignment 概念存在同义反复风险，实验覆盖面（架构/种子/sparsity 范围）不足以支撑核心主张的一般性。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.0/5 | 梯度分析定性正确但 coupling alignment 框架存在循环论证；headline 19% PPL 数字在非匹配 sparsity 下报告；adaptive penalty 在 30% 时 λ 触顶 5000 |\n| Excitement | 3.5/5 | 第一篇系统研究训练目标如何影响 SwiGLU sparsity prediction 质量的论文；target-swap 消融设计精巧且结果有说服力 |\n| Reproducibility | 4.0/5 | 超参完整列出（§4.1, Appendix B-C）；3-seed 评估覆盖核心实验；训练步数/硬件/数据明确 |\n| Clarity | 3.5/5 | 写作整体清晰，2D taxonomy (Table 1) 有效组织 related work；但 §3.7 coupling alignment 定义不够形式化 |\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n**S1. 问题定位精准**：首次系统质疑\"为什么 sparsity predictor 都用 BCE\"，并提出输出分布蒸馏作为替代。这填补了 Table 1 中 Per-token × Coupled 的空白，定位明确且有研究价值。\n\n**S2. Target-swap 消融设计巧妙**：C1 实验（§4.4, Table 3）将 mask 质量与训练机制解耦——用 BCE 训练在 KL-derived targets 上，复现了 KL 的性能（50% 时 Δ≤0.79pp，30% retrained seeds Δ≤1.18pp）。这是全文最有说服力的实验证据，直接回答了\"是 mask 更好还是训练过程更好\"的关键因果问题。\n\n**S3. 实验覆盖面（核心配置）**：在主要实验（LLaMA-3.1-8B, 30% uniform allocation）上提供了 3-seed 评估（PPL CV=0.2%，Appendix F），包含 6 个 downstream task。附录涵盖了 bottleneck 灵敏度（Appendix B）、温度退火（Appendix B.1）、训练收敛（B.2）、OOD 评估（Appendix D）和 compensation redundancy（Appendix J），thoroughness 值得肯定。\n\n**S4. 诚实的 Limitations**：论文坦承 50% sparsity downstream collapse（§4.2, line 517-520）、cross-architecture 为\"preliminary single-seed\"（§4.6）、以及缺乏 wall-clock speedup（Limitations section）。这种科学诚实度是加分项。\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] Coupling alignment 概念存在同义反复（tautological）风险\n- 具体位置：§3.7 (lines 392-444), §5 Conclusion\n- 问题：论文将 coupling alignment 定义为\"训练损失是否优化一个跨层组合的量\"（line 426-428），然后论证\"只有直接优化 next-token prediction loss 的目标才能发现产生好 next-token prediction 的 mask\"。这在逻辑上接近同义反复——用输出级别目标训练当然倾向于优化输出级别指标。\n- C1 消融部分缓解了这一问题（证明 mask 质量可迁移），但论文同时主张\"gradient coupling remains necessary at the discovery stage\"（line 572-573），这又回到了循环论证：KL 之所以发现好 mask，是因为它的梯度耦合了所有层；好 mask 之所以好，是因为它们优化了输出分布。论文没有提供独立于 PPL/downstream 的 mask 质量定义来打破这个循环。\n- 为什么影响接收：这是论文的核心理论贡献（§3.7 是独立的 subsection），如果概念本身是循环的，论文就退化为\"KL 比 BCE 效果好\"的纯实证观察，概念框架不成立。\n- 建议：(a) 提供 coupling alignment 的可操作形式化定义（如用 mask 的梯度协方差矩阵秩来量化 coupling 程度）；(b) 或者降低 coupling alignment 的理论抱负，明确承认这是描述性分类而非因果机制；(c) 测试一个 intermediate coupling 级别的目标（如 hidden-state KL 而非 output KL）来验证\"越 coupled 越好\"的梯度预测。\n\n**N2** [critical] Headline 19% PPL improvement 在非匹配 sparsity 下报告\n- 具体位置：Abstract (line 012), §4.2 (lines 491-494)\n- 问题：Abstract 称\"reduces perplexity by 19% relative to TEAL\"（10.82→8.81），但 KL predictor 实际达到 28.5% sparsity vs TEAL 的 ~30%。在匹配 sparsity（28.5%）下，改善为 14.5%（§4.2 line 494 括号内承认）。Abstract 突出 19% 而将 14.5% 藏在正文括号里，构成对读者的误导。\n- 进一步地，TEAL 在 \"2700 steps\" 行（Table 2 footnote †）显示 TEAL global 2700 steps 的 PPL 也用于比较——但 TEAL 是 training-free 的，不需要 2700 步训练，所以\"2700 steps\"行的意义不明确。\n- 为什么影响接收：Headline claim 的准确性直接影响论文的可信度。ARR reviewers 对 overclaimed numbers 极其敏感。\n- 建议：Abstract 使用匹配 sparsity 下的 14.5% 数字，或明确标注\"at 28.5% actual sparsity vs. TEAL's 30%\"。\n\n**N3** [critical] 50% sparsity 下 downstream 全面崩溃，严重限制方法的实用价值\n- 具体位置：§4.2 (lines 515-520), Table 2, Table 14\n- 问题：50% sparsity 下所有方法 MMLU≈28%（≈random），GSM8K<2.2%（≈0%），ARC-c 33.8。论文正确指出这是\"capacity-limited regime\"，但这意味着：\n  - 方法的 unambiguous wins 仅在 30% sparsity，且主要体现在 PPL 而非 downstream tasks\n  - 即使在 30%，knowledge-intensive tasks（MMLU, GSM8K）也不占优：MMLU 40.7 vs TEAL 41.2（BCE global），GSM8K 7.5 vs 8.0 (BCE global)\n  - 论文声称的\"19% PPL improvement\"对应的 downstream 实际是 mixed：commonsense reasoning 赢（ARC +4.4pp, HellaSwag +7.8pp），knowledge-intensive 输（MMLU −5.2pp）\n- 为什么影响接收：如果方法只在一个 sparsity level 的 PPL 上有优势，而 downstream 是 task-dependent 的，核心价值大打折扣。读者会问：为什么不直接用 TEAL（training-free, no predictor）？\n- 建议：(a) 在 abstract 和 introduction 中更平衡地呈现 downstream 结果，而非仅突出 PPL + commonsense wins；(b) 分析为什么 KL 的 mode-covering property 损害 knowledge tasks（§4.2 lines 503-510 的假设值得展开）；(c) 考虑 task-specific 目标的可行性作为 future work。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] Cross-architecture 证据过于薄弱\n- 具体位置：§4.6 (lines 624-635), Appendix K (Table 13), Appendix N (Tables 15-16)\n- 问题：Mistral-7B 和 Qwen-2.5-14B 均为 single seed 评估。Qwen-14B 结果尤其令人担忧：30% sparsity 下 GSM8K 从 87.57（dense）降至 48.82（KL global），MMLU 从 79.77 降至 63.72；14B TEAL 保留 dense reasoning 的 59% vs KL 的 37%（line 630-631），这反转了 8B 上的优势。论文将此归为\"scale-dependent effects\"，但没有解释机制。\n- 建议：至少在 Mistral-7B 上补充 3-seed 评估（训练成本仅 ~45min×3）；或明确标注\"cross-architecture results are preliminary and single-seed, conclusions may not generalize\"。\n\n**W2** [warning] MSE baseline 为 single seed，且 λ_max 不匹配\n- 具体位置：Table 2 (30%/50% MSE rows), Appendix G (lines 1175-1197)\n- 问题：MSE baseline 使用 λ_max=1000（vs KL 的 5000），且为 single seed。尽管 1.9×/9.6× over TEAL 的差距大到 seed sensitivity 不太可能改变结论，但 MSE 是 coupling alignment taxonomy 中 \"intermediate\" 类别的唯一代表——如果 MSE 的超参不是最优的（λ_max 不同），这影响了 decomposed vs intermediate vs coupled 的对比公平性。Appendix G 确认 MSE 在 27.7% actual sparsity 下 PPL=20.43，但这是否因为 λ_max=1000 导致 sparsity 未充分收敛（30% target 只到 27.7%）？\n- 建议：统一 λ_max 或说明为什么 MSE 需要不同的 λ_max。\n\n**W3** [warning] Adaptive penalty controller 在 30% 时 λ 触顶 5000，暴露控制器设计缺陷\n- 具体位置：§3.5 (lines 344-356), Appendix C Table 10\n- 问题：Table 10 显示 30% target 时 λ=5000（cap），actual sparsity=28.5%（deviation=5.0%）。这意味着：(a) 控制器已经用尽了惩罚力度但仍未达标；(b) 如果目标是 25% 或更低，控制器可能完全失效。这暗示 predictor 的自然 sparsity（无惩罚时）显著高于 30%，几何倍增策略在低 sparsity 区间设计不当。\n- 与之对比，50% target 时 λ 仅为 8，deviation=0.8%——控制器在高 sparsity 时工作良好但在低 sparsity 时表现差。\n- 建议：讨论 λ_max 敏感度，报告 λ_max=10000 或 20000 时的结果；或使用 PID 控制器替代几何倍增。\n\n**W4** [warning] BCE+Gumbel 比 standard BCE 更差（32.22 vs 27.97），削弱了组件贡献的因果推断\n- 具体位置：Table 4, §4.5 (lines 592-604)\n- 问题：论文的核心方法是 \"KL + Gumbel\"。消融显示 BCE+Gumbel (32.22) 比 BCE without Gumbel (27.97) 差 4.25 PPL。论文解释 Gumbel 的随机采样破坏了 BCE 的 binary target signal，这合理但引出一个对比公平性问题：Table 2 中 KL+Gumbel (11.79) vs BCE without Gumbel (27.97) 不是 apples-to-apples，因为 relaxation 策略不同。\n- 更深层的问题：如果 Gumbel 对 BCE 有害但对 KL 有利，这是否暗示 KL 的优势部分来自与 Gumbel 的 interaction 而非 coupling alignment 本身？论文声称\"the loss effect (ΔPPL=20.4) is 5.1× the relaxation effect (ΔPPL=3.98)\"（lines 603-604），但这种加法分解假设两个效应独立，而 BCE+Gumbel 的恶化恰恰说明它们有交互作用。\n- 建议：报告 KL+STE 对 BCE+STE 的对比（Table 4 已有 KL+STE=15.77，需补充 BCE+STE），以控制 relaxation 策略。或正式承认 KL 和 Gumbel 存在正向交互效应，量化其大小。\n\n**W5** [warning] 梯度分析（§3.6）定性有效但缺乏定量支撑\n- 具体位置：§3.6 (lines 357-391), Appendix H\n- 问题：§3.6 的梯度对比是论文方法论贡献的核心之一。BCE 梯度 ∂L/∂s_j = σ(s_j) − m*_j 只依赖 neuron j——这正确。KL 梯度引入 downstream Jacobian ∂z_sparse/∂m̃_j^(ℓ)——这也正确。但论文的推理链是：\n  1. KL 梯度 coupled → 2. 发现 compositionally superior mask patterns → 3. 解释了 PPL 优势\n  - Step 1→2 没有直接证据（coupled gradient 为什么 discovers better masks？可能只是 KL 的 loss landscape 更好？）\n  - SNR 分析（Figure 4）显示 BCE SNR 2.3× higher than KL，论文解释为\"target alignment not gradient clarity\"——但 SNR 本身不能区分\"gradient direction 更好\"和\"gradient 更 coupled\"\n  - 缺少：per-layer gradient 相关性分析（验证 KL gradient 确实在层间传播信号），或 Jacobian 条件数分析\n- 建议：至少补充一个实验直接度量 gradient coupling 的强度（如计算 ∂loss/∂m_j^(ℓ) 对不同层 m_k^(ℓ') 的偏导数范数比），与 BCE 对比。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **m1**: Table 2 中 \"BCE global†\" 的 † 标注定义在 footnote 中（\"BCE global (2700 steps)\"），但 Table 2 本身的 † 在左下角，容易与其他 footnote 混淆。\n- **m2**: 引用验证发现 `de2024griffin` (Griffin) 在 OpenAlex 中未找到匹配（可能是 arXiv 预印本尚未索引），不影响引用正确性但需确认 venue 信息完整。\n- **m3**: §3.3 line 305 \"We anneal τ linearly from 1.0 to 0.1 over training\" — Appendix B.1 Table 8 显示固定 τ=0.5 性能更好（11.861 vs 11.963），但正文选择了 linear annealing 作为 default。这不影响结论（<1.8% PPL variation）但应在正文中提及为何选择非最优 default。\n- **m4**: 论文第 94 行明确声明\"primarily an empirical study\"，但 §3.6-3.7 的 gradient analysis 和 coupling alignment taxonomy 试图做理论贡献。这种定位不一致可能让 reviewer 期望管理混乱——如果是实证研究，theoretical framework 不应作为 contribution claim。\n- **m5**: Figure 2 的 y 轴使用 log scale 但标注为线性刻度值（6, 8, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50, 100），视觉上夸大了低 sparsity 区间的差异。\n\n---\n\n## 缺失引用\n\n- **Wortsman et al. (2023)** \"Small-scale proxies for large-scale Transformer training instabilities\" — 与 training stability / SNR analysis 相关\n- **Dettmers et al. (2024)** \"SpQR\" 或其他 quantization + sparsity 交叉工作 — 论文完全未讨论 sparsity 与 quantization 的交互\n- **Clark et al. (2020)** \"ELECTRA\" — 作为另一种使用 binary classification 训练但目标不同的对比（虽然不是直接相关，但 replaced token detection 的 BCE 训练思路可提供对比视角）\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\nQ1. Table 3 的 C1 消融证明了 mask 质量可迁移，但如果反过来——用 KL 训练在 magnitude-based oracle targets 上——性能如何？这将测试 KL 的 coupled gradient 是否在 suboptimal targets 上也有优势。\n\nQ2. 30% sparsity 下 λ 触顶 5000 且 actual sparsity=28.5%。如果将 λ_max 提高到 50000，能否达到 30%？如果能，PPL 是否会因过强的惩罚而恶化？\n\nQ3. 论文主张 forward KL 优于 reverse KL 是因为 mode-covering（§3.4, §4.5 Table 4: forward KL 11.79 vs reverse KL 12.80）。但 mode-covering 也解释了为什么 knowledge-intensive tasks（MMLU, GSM8K）表现差——forward KL 分散概率质量到所有 mode 上，而 knowledge 需要 concentrated probability on correct answers。作者是否考虑过 task-specific 的 KL 方向选择？\n\nQ4. SPON 的正交性（Appendix O, cosine similarity 0.196, R²=0.038）显示 SPON bias correction 和 KL predictor 信号近乎独立。但 SPON 只贡献 2.4% 改善。这是否意味着 static bias correction 在 KL distillation 框架下几乎无用？\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\n本文提出了一个有价值的实证发现：用 KL 蒸馏替代 BCE 训练 SwiGLU 稀疏性预测器，能发现更优的 mask pattern。Target-swap 消融（C1）是最有说服力的证据，直接隔离了 mask 质量的贡献。\n\n主要不足在三个层面：(1) **理论层面**——coupling alignment 框架接近同义反复，gradient analysis 缺乏定量支撑；(2) **实验层面**——headline 19% 在非匹配 sparsity 下报告，cross-architecture 证据过薄（single seed, 14B 结果反转），MSE baseline 超参不匹配；(3) **实用层面**——50% sparsity downstream 崩溃，30% downstream gains 是 task-dependent 的，缺乏 wall-clock speedup 验证。\n\n论文作为分析性实证研究有贡献（类似于 ablation study + negative result 的价值），但 coupling alignment 的理论抱负与实验支撑之间存在 gap。如果降低理论 claim 并修复 headline number 的准确性，适合 Findings 或 borderline Conference。",
  "reviewer_id": "1",
  "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu"
}

predictor）？\n- 建议：(a) 在 abstract 和 introduction 中更平衡地呈现 downstream 结果，而非仅突出 PPL + commonsense wins；(b) 分析为什么 KL 的 mode-covering property 损害 knowledge tasks（§4.2 lines 503-510 的假设值得展开）；(c) 考虑 task-specific 目标的可行性作为 future work。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] Cross-architecture 证据过于薄弱\n- 具体位置：§4.6 (lines 624-635), Appendix K (Table 13), Appendix N (Tables 15-16)\n- 问题：Mistral-7B 和 Qwen-2.5-14B 均为 single seed 评估。Qwen-14B 结果尤其令人担忧：30% sparsity 下 GSM8K 从 87.57（dense）降至 48.82（KL global），MMLU 从 79.77 降至 63.72；14B TEAL 保留 dense reasoning 的 59% vs KL 的 37%（line 630-631），这反转了 8B 上的优势。论文将此归为\"scale-dependent effects\"，但没有解释机制。\n- 建议：至少在 Mistral-7B 上补充 3-seed 评估（训练成本仅 ~45min×3）；或明确标注\"cross-architecture results are preliminary and single-seed, conclusions may not generalize\"。\n\n**W2** [warning] MSE baseline 为 single seed，且 λ_max 不匹配\n- 具体位置：Table 2 (30%/50% MSE rows), Appendix G (lines 1175-1197)\n- 问题：MSE baseline 使用 λ_max=1000（vs KL 的 5000），且为 single seed。尽管 1.9×/9.6× over TEAL 的差距大到 seed sensitivity 不太可能改变结论，但 MSE 是 coupling alignment taxonomy 中 \"intermediate\" 类别的唯一代表——如果 MSE 的超参不是最优的（λ_max 不同），这影响了 decomposed vs intermediate vs coupled 的对比公平性。Appendix G 确认 MSE 在 27.7% actual sparsity 下 PPL=20.43，但这是否因为 λ_max=1000 导致 sparsity 未充分收敛（30% target 只到 27.7%）？\n- 建议：统一 λ_max 或说明为什么 MSE 需要不同的 λ_max。\n\n**W3** [warning] Adaptive penalty controller 在 30% 时 λ 触顶 5000，暴露控制器设计缺陷\n- 具体位置：§3.5 (lines 344-356), Appendix C Table 10\n- 问题：Table 10 显示 30% target 时 λ=5000（cap），actual sparsity=28.5%（deviation=5.0%）。这意味着：(a) 控制器已经用尽了惩罚力度但仍未达标；(b) 如果目标是 25% 或更低，控制器可能完全失效。这暗示 predictor 的自然 sparsity（无惩罚时）显著高于 30%，几何倍增策略在低 sparsity 区间设计不当。\n- 与之对比，50% target 时 λ 仅为 8，deviation=0.8%——控制器在高 sparsity 时工作良好但在低 sparsity 时表现差。\n- 建议：讨论 λ_max 敏感度，报告 λ_max=10000 或 20000 时的结果；或使用 PID 控制器替代几何倍增。\n\n**W4** [warning] BCE+Gumbel 比 standard BCE 更差（32.22 vs 27.97），削弱了组件贡献的因果推断\n- 具体位置：Table 4, §4.5 (lines 592-604)\n- 问题：论文的核心方法是 \"KL + Gumbel\"。消融显示 BCE+Gumbel (32.22) 比 BCE without Gumbel (27.97) 差 4.25 PPL。论文解释 Gumbel 的随机采样破坏了 BCE 的 binary target signal，这合理但引出一个对比公平性问题：Table 2 中 KL+Gumbel (11.79) vs BCE without Gumbel (27.97) 不是 apples-to-apples，因为 relaxation 策略不同。\n- 更深层的问题：如果 Gumbel 对 BCE 有害但对 KL 有利，这是否暗示 KL 的优势部分来自与 Gumbel 的 interaction 而非 coupling alignment 本身？论文声称\"the loss effect (ΔPPL=20.4) is 5.1× the relaxation effect (ΔPPL=3.98)\"（lines 603-604），但这种加法分解假设两个效应独立，而 BCE+Gumbel 的恶化恰恰说明它们有交互作用。\n- 建议：报告 KL+STE 对 BCE+STE 的对比（Table 4 已有 KL+STE=15.77，需补充 BCE+STE），以控制 relaxation 策略。或正式承认 KL 和 Gumbel 存在正向交互效应，量化其大小。\n\n**W5** [warning] 梯度分析（§3.6）定性有效但缺乏定量支撑\n- 具体位置：§3.6 (lines 357-391), Appendix H\n- 问题：§3.6 的梯度对比是论文方法论贡献的核心之一。BCE 梯度 ∂L/∂s_j = σ(s_j) − m*_j 只依赖 neuron j——这正确。KL 梯度引入 downstream Jacobian ∂z_sparse/∂m̃_j^(ℓ)——这也正确。但论文的推理链是：\n  1. KL 梯度 coupled → 2. 发现 compositionally superior mask patterns → 3. 解释了 PPL 优势\n  - Step 1→2 没有直接证据（coupled gradient 为什么 discovers better masks？可能只是 KL 的 loss landscape 更好？）\n  - SNR 分析（Figure 4）显示 BCE SNR 2.3× higher than KL，论文解释为\"target alignment not gradient clarity\"——但 SNR 本身不能区分\"gradient direction 更好\"和\"gradient 更 coupled\"\n  - 缺少：per-layer gradient 相关性分析（验证 KL gradient 确实在层间传播信号），或 Jacobian 条件数分析\n- 建议：至少补充一个实验直接度量 gradient coupling 的强度（如计算 ∂loss/∂m_j^(ℓ) 对不同层 m_k^(ℓ') 的偏导数范数比），与 BCE 对比。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **m1**: Table 2 中 \"BCE global†\" 的 † 标注定义在 footnote 中（\"BCE global (2700 steps)\"），但 Table 2 本身的 † 在左下角，容易与其他 footnote 混淆。\n- **m2**: 引用验证发现 `de2024griffin` (Griffin) 在 OpenAlex 中未找到匹配（可能是 arXiv 预印本尚未索引），不影响引用正确性但需确认 venue 信息完整。\n- **m3**: §3.3 line 305 \"We anneal τ linearly from 1.0 to 0.1 over training\" — Appendix B.1 Table 8 显示固定 τ=0.5 性能更好（11.861 vs 11.963），但正文选择了 linear annealing 作为 default。这不影响结论（<1.8% PPL variation）但应在正文中提及为何选择非最优 default。\n- **m4**: 论文第 94 行明确声明\"primarily an empirical study\"，但 §3.6-3.7 的 gradient analysis 和 coupling alignment taxonomy 试图做理论贡献。这种定位不一致可能让 reviewer 期望管理混乱——如果是实证研究，theoretical framework 不应作为 contribution claim。\n- **m5**: Figure 2 的 y 轴使用 log scale 但标注为线性刻度值（6, 8, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50, 100），视觉上夸大了低 sparsity 区间的差异。\n\n---\n\n## 缺失引用\n\n- **Wortsman et al. (2023)** \"Small-scale proxies for large-scale Transformer training instabilities\" — 与 training stability / SNR analysis 相关\n- **Dettmers et al. (2024)** \"SpQR\" 或其他 quantization + sparsity 交叉工作 — 论文完全未讨论 sparsity 与 quantization 的交互\n- **Clark et al. (2020)** \"ELECTRA\" — 作为另一种使用 binary classification 训练但目标不同的对比（虽然不是直接相关，但 replaced token detection 的 BCE 训练思路可提供对比视角）\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\nQ1. Table 3 的 C1 消融证明了 mask 质量可迁移，但如果反过来——用 KL 训练在 magnitude-based oracle targets 上——性能如何？这将测试 KL 的 coupled gradient 是否在 suboptimal targets 上也有优势。\n\nQ2. 30% sparsity 下 λ 触顶 5000 且 actual sparsity=28.5%。如果将 λ_max 提高到 50000，能否达到 30%？如果能，PPL 是否会因过强的惩罚而恶化？\n\nQ3. 论文主张 forward KL 优于 reverse KL 是因为 mode-covering（§3.4, §4.5 Table 4: forward KL 11.79 vs reverse KL 12.80）。但 mode-covering 也解释了为什么 knowledge-intensive tasks（MMLU, GSM8K）表现差——forward KL 分散概率质量到所有 mode 上，而 knowledge 需要 concentrated probability on correct answers。作者是否考虑过 task-specific 的 KL 方向选择？\n\nQ4. SPON 的正交性（Appendix O, cosine similarity 0.196, R²=0.038）显示 SPON bias correction 和 KL predictor 信号近乎独立。但 SPON 只贡献 2.4% 改善。这是否意味着 static bias correction 在 KL distillation 框架下几乎无用？\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\n本文提出了一个有价值的实证发现：用 KL 蒸馏替代 BCE 训练 SwiGLU 稀疏性预测器，能发现更优的 mask pattern。Target-swap 消融（C1）是最有说服力的证据，直接隔离了 mask 质量的贡献。\n\n主要不足在三个层面：(1) **理论层面**——coupling alignment 框架接近同义反复，gradient analysis 缺乏定量支撑；(2) **实验层面**——headline 19% 在非匹配 sparsity 下报告，cross-architecture 证据过薄（single seed, 14B 结果反转），MSE baseline 超参不匹配；(3) **实用层面**——50% sparsity downstream 崩溃，30% downstream gains 是 task-dependent 的，缺乏 wall-clock speedup 验证。\n\n论文作为分析性实证研究有贡献（类似于 ablation study + negative result 的价值），但 coupling alignment 的理论抱负与实验支撑之间存在 gap。如果降低理论 claim 并修复 headline number 的准确性，适合 Findings 或 borderline Conference。",

**核心结论**: Overall 3.5/5 (Borderline Conference)

**3 个 Must-Fix**:
1. Coupling alignment 框架存在同义反复风险——"output-level 目标发现好的 output-level mask" 近乎循环论证，缺乏独立的形式化定义

3. 50% sparsity downstream 全面崩溃至 near-random，方法的 unambiguous wins 实质上仅限于 30% sparsity 的 PPL

**5 个 Should-Fix**:
- Cross-architecture 证据过薄（single seed, 14B GSM8K 反转 -20pp）
- MSE baseline λ_max 不匹配（1000 vs 5000）且 single seed
- Adaptive penalty 在 30% 时 λ 触顶 5000，控制器设计存疑
- BCE+Gumbel 比 standard BCE 更差，KL 与 Gumbel 存在未量化的交互效应
- 梯度分析（§3.6）定性有效但缺乏直接度量 gradient coupling 强度的定量实验

以下是 Reviewer 2 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每个 Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Minor 问题标注 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]，并说明理由。如果 [REBUT]，请引用论文中的具体 Section/Table/Figure 作为证据。

---

## Reviewer 2 审稿报告

**Overall**: 3.0/5 (Findings)
**Confidence**: 4/5

### Must-Fix

**N1** [critical] 与 MaskLLM 的新颖性边界不清晰
- MaskLLM (NeurIPS 2024) 已使用 frozen weights + KL divergence + Gumbel relaxation + learnable masks 的完整框架。论文三点区分（sparsity type, mask generation, allocation）更像 application domain 差异而非方法论创新。核心 insight "KL couples gradients" 在 MaskLLM 中已隐含成立。
- 建议：明确论证 global allocation 场景的新挑战；提供 MaskLLM 中 KL vs BCE gap 的定量对比。

**N2** [critical] "Coupling Alignment" 概念缺少形式化，过度包装已知原理
- 本质上是"end-to-end training outperforms component-wise training when components interact"——深度学习基本原理。Taxonomy (Table 1) 填充空格不构成理论发现。
- 建议：定位为"对已知原理在 sparsity prediction 场景的经验验证"，降低 claim 强度。

### Should-Fix

**W1** 实际可用 regime 过窄：30% 是唯一有意义 regime，但 MMLU 仍大幅退化（65.27%→40.7%），TEAL 反而更好（45.86%）。加 6.4% latency overhead 无实际加速。
**W2** 跨架构/跨规模结果过于初步：Mistral single-seed，14B MMLU -2.79pp、GSM8K -20pp，但 Abstract/Conclusion 仍作为 contribution。
**W3** Abstract 数字选择性呈现：只展示 KL 胜出任务，MMLU -5.2pp 仅给标签，未提及绝对退化（65.3%→40.7%）。
**W4** Training data 单一性：所有 predictor 仅在 WikiText-103 训练，C4 OOD 仅报告 PPL 未报告 downstream，C4 cross-seed variance 增大（CV 6.3%）。

### Minor
- M1: 30% λ 达 cap (5000)，最实用 regime 表现最差
- M2: MSE λ_max=1000 vs KL 5000 未控制
- M3: Figure 2 caption "82% at 70%" 但 70% downstream 不可用
- M4: "proximate quality driver" 反复出现
- M5: xu2026spon year=2026 但引为 ICML 2025
- M6: de2024griffin OpenAlex not_found

### Missing References
1. "A Sparsity Predicting Approach via Activation Pattern Clustering" (Dhar et al., 2025)
2. "Universal Properties of Activation Sparsity in Modern LLMs" (arXiv:2509.00454)
3. §2 "All prior predictor-based methods use decomposed or intermediate objectives" 需更精确限定

### Excitement: 2.5/5 — 经验性发现有价值但属 incremental；MaskLLM 已用 KL+Gumbel；无 wall-clock speedup；可用 regime 仅 30%。

### Questions
1. MaskLLM 中 KL vs BCE 的 PPL gap 与本文 2.4× gap 可比吗？
2. 30% 下有无方法恢复 MMLU 差距？
3. Gumbel 对 KL 有利但对 BCE 有害——为什么？

TEAL's narrower activation preservation\"（line 015），不提绝对值退化幅度。这种不对称呈现是 overclaiming 的典型模式。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W4: Training data 单一性**\n\n这是我审稿中遗漏的有效问题。补充：\n\n- C4 OOD 评估（Appendix D, Table 11）仅报告 PPL，未报告任何 downstream task。如果 predictor 过拟合 WikiText-103 的 activation patterns（论文自己在 Limitations line 698-700 承认\"partially overfits to training-domain activation patterns\"），那 downstream OOD 性能可能比 PPL OOD 更差。\n- C4 cross-seed variance CV=6.3% vs WikiText-2 CV=0.2%——30× 增大——说明 predictor 的 OOD robustness 存在问题。\n- Seed 42 的 C4 PPL (18.22) 远高于其他 seeds (16.37, 16.36)——这与 seed 42 在其他实验中的异常值模式一致（Table 3 的 s42 C1 也来自不同 run），暗示存在 seed-specific overfitting。\n\n建议在 C4 上也报告至少 ARC-c 和 MMLU 的 downstream performance。\n\n---\n\n### Minor\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M1**: λ 达 cap 在最实用 regime——与我的 W3 一致。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M2**: MSE λ_max 不匹配——与我的 W2 一致。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M3**: Figure 2 标注 \"82% at 70%\" 但 70% downstream 完全不可用（Table 14: 50% 已 collapse），纯 PPL 比较在实用角度无意义。这个标注对读者有误导性——暗示方法在 70% 仍有价值。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M4**: \"proximate quality driver\" 在 §4.4 (line 571), §5 (line 644), Abstract (line 023) 反复出现。风格问题但确实冗余。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M5**: xu2026spon 的 bib key 标为 2026 但论文发表于 ICML 2025。引用验证也确认 matched_year=2025。应修正 bib 年份。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-M6**: de2024griffin 在 OpenAlex 未找到——与我的 m2 一致。\n\n---\n\n### Missing References\n\n**[NEUTRAL] R2-Missing-1/2**: 我无法验证 \"Dhar et al., 2025\" 和 \"arXiv:2509.00454\" 的存在性和相关性。如果确实存在，应当引用。\n\n**[AGREE] R2-Missing-3**: §2 line 172-173 \"All prior predictor-based methods use decomposed (BCE) or intermediate (MSE) objectives\" 需要更精确限定范围（如 \"for activation sparsity prediction\"），因为 MaskLLM 已在 weight sparsity 场景使用 KL。\n\n---\n\n### Excitement 评分\n\nR2 给 Excitement 2.5/5，我给 3.5/5。分歧在于：\n\nR2 认为 MaskLLM 已用 KL+Gumbel，所以本文是 incremental。如上述 REBUT 所论，我认为 activation sparsity + predictor + global allocation 是不同问题。但我同意 coupling alignment 框架的理论贡献有限（本质上是已知原理的经验验证）。综合来看，我的 Excitement 评分保持 3.5——核心实证发现（KL-derived masks diverge 46-68% from magnitude yet produce lower PPL）和 C1 ablation 的实验设计有足够新颖性，但不足以到 4.0。\n\n---\n\n### Reviewer 2 Questions 的补充\n\n**Q1 (MaskLLM KL vs BCE gap)**: 好问题。MaskLLM 论文未报告 KL vs BCE 对比（只用了 KL）。如果 MaskLLM 中 KL vs BCE gap 很小，这会削弱本文的 coupling alignment 论证——因为 coupling 在 N:M constrained 场景也应有效果。作者应尝试在 MaskLLM 设置下复现 KL vs BCE 对比，或至少讨论为什么 gap 在 global allocation 下可能更大。\n\n**Q2 (30% MMLU recovery)**: 我在自己的 Q3 中也问了相关问题。Forward KL 的 mode-covering 性质可能是 MMLU 退化的根因——保留了过多的 activation patterns 导致 knowledge concentration 不足。\n\n**Q3 (Gumbel+KL vs Gumbel+BCE interaction)**: 我在自己的 W4 中详细讨论了这个问题。Table 4 的数据清晰显示 Gumbel 对 KL 有利（+relaxation enables gradient flow through mask）但对 BCE 有害（stochastic sampling corrupts binary target signal）。论文的\"加法分解\"（loss effect 5.1× relaxation effect）假设效应独立，但 BCE+Gumbel 的恶化证明存在交互效应。这是方法论上的一个 gap。\n\n---\n\n### 综合判断\n\nR2 总体给 3.0 (Findings)，我给 3.5 (Borderline Conference)。在审阅 R2 的意见后，我认为：\n\n- R2-N1 (MaskLLM novelty) 的主要论点我不完全同意——activation sparsity prediction 与 weight sparsity optimization 你是 Reviewer 1，负责审查 **方法严谨性 + 实验设计**。

## Venue 信息
- **会议**：EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR May 2026 cycle 提交）
- **截止日期**：2026-05-25
- **页数限制**：正文 8 页，参考文献不限，Limitations/Ethics 不计入页数
- **模板**：ACL style（\usepackage[review]{acl}）
- **匿名规则**：Double-blind
- **附录**：在 references 之后，审稿人可参考

## 论文路径
- LaTeX 源码：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.tex`
- PDF：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.pdf`

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 技术正确性
- §3.6 梯度分析（BCE vs KL gradient structure）的数学正确性
- KL divergence 公式（Eq.5）的 forward KL 方向选择是否充分论证
- Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation（Eq.4）的温度退火策略是否合理
- Adaptive Penalty Controller（Eq.7）的收敛性论证

### 2. 实验设计严谨性
- **Table 1 (Main Results)**：KL vs TEAL vs BCE 对比条件是否公平（sparsity mismatch: KL 28.5% vs TEAL 30%）
- **Table 2 (C1 Target-Swap)**：ablation 是否充分隔离了 target quality vs gradient coupling 效应
- **Table 3 (Ablation)**：50% sparsity only — 是否需要 30% ablation 数据
- **Multi-seed 统计**：3-seed 是否足够；哪些结果是 single-seed（MSE baseline, 50% global）
- **MSE baseline**：λ_max=1000 vs KL 5000，single seed — 对比公平性
- **数据一致性**：表格间数字是否一致；limitations.tex 中 PPL 数值与正文表格是否匹配

### 3. 实验覆盖度
- 仅 WikiText-103 训练、WikiText-2 评估 — OOD 泛化性是否充分
- 30%/50% 两个 sparsity level 是否足够；40% gap
- 跨架构 Mistral/Qwen 证据厚度（seed 数量、downstream 覆盖度）

### 4. 格式合规
- 正文是否严格控制在 8 页内
- Double-blind 是否违规（代码链接、GPU 型号等去匿名化风险）
- 引用格式是否符合 ACL natbib 规范

请按 ACL Reviewer Guidelines 的标准输出审稿报告，包含：Summary (2-3句) → Strengths (逐条) → Weaknesses (逐条，标注 severity: critical/warning/minor) → Questions → Missing References → Overall Assessment (1-5 分制，参照 EMNLP 2026 评分标准) → Confidence (1-5)。

_source line 877 · 2026-05-20T15:25:20.051298+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form – Complete Rating Dimensions

## Primary Rating Dimensions

**Reviewer Confidence**
- Scale: 1–5
- "5 = Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- "1 = Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

**Soundness**
- Scale: 1–5 (with half-point increments)
- "5 = Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- "1 = Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

**Excitement**
- Scale: 1–5 (with half-point increments)
- "5 = Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others"
- "1 = Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me"

**Overall Assessment**
- Scale: 1–5 (with half-point increments)
- "5 = Consider for Award: outstanding paper award" (top 2.5%)
- "4 = Conference: could be accepted to *ACL conference"
- "3 = Findings: could be accepted to Findings of the ACL"
- "2 = Resubmit next cycle"
- "1 = Do not resubmit"

## Secondary Assessment Dimensions

**Reproducibility**: 1–5 scale addressing result replicability
**Datasets**: 1–5 scale for dataset value to others
**Software**: 1–5 scale for software utility
**Author Identity Knowledge**: 1–5 scale for anonymity assessment

_source line 907 · 2026-05-20T15:25:57.020334+00:00_

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	We present a KL distillation framework for training contextual sparsity predictors in SwiGLU-based language models (Figure~\ref{fig:overview}).
5	The method replaces per-neuron binary classification (BCE) with an output-distribution objective that couples all pruning decisions through the model's forward pass and achieves competitive performance without auxiliary error compensation networks at inference.
6	
7	\subsection{Problem Formulation}
8	\label{sec:method:formulation}
9	
10	Consider a pretrained transformer with $L$ layers, each containing a SwiGLU feed-forward network~\cite{shazeer2020glu}.
11	For layer $\ell$, the FFN computes:
12	\begin{align}
13	\mathbf{y}^{(\ell)} = \bigl(\sigma(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}) \odot \mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)}\bigr) \mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)},
14	\label{eq:swiglu}
15	\end{align}
16	where $\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d}$ is the hidden state after attention ($d{=}4096$), $\sigma$ denotes SiLU, $\mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}, \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d_{\text{ff}}}$ and $\mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{\text{ff}} \times d}$ are the gate, up-projection, and down-projection matrices, and $d_{\text{ff}}{=}14{,}336$.
17	
18	Define the gate activation $\mathbf{g}^{(\ell)} = \sigma(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_g^{(\ell)}) \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{\text{ff}}}$.
19	Each element $g_j^{(\ell)}$ determines the contribution of neuron $j$ to the output.
20	We introduce a binary mask $\mathbf{m}^{(\ell)} \in \{0,1\}^{d_{\text{ff}}}$ that zeros out pruned neurons:
21	\begin{align}
22	\mathbf{y}_{\text{sparse}}^{(\ell)} = \bigl(\mathbf{m}^{(\ell)} \odot \mathbf{g}^{(\ell)} \odot \mathbf{x}^{(\ell)} \mathbf{W}_u^{(\ell)}\bigr) \mathbf{W}_d^{(\ell)}.
23	\label{eq:sparse_ffn}
24	\end{align}
25	Our goal is to learn a mask predictor $f_\theta^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{x}^{(\ell)}) \to \mathbf{s}^{(\ell)} \in (0,1)^{d_{\text{ff}}}$ that produces per-neuron importan
…[truncated 11547 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
5	
6	\paragraph{Model and Predictor.}
7	We evaluate on LLaMA-3.1-8B~\cite{grattafiori2024llama3}, which uses SwiGLU FFN blocks~\cite{shazeer2020glu} with $d_{\text{model}}=4096$ and $d_{\text{ff}}=14336$ across 32 layers.
8	The sparsity predictor is a two-layer MLP with 2.37M parameters per layer (75.9M total), implemented as Linear($4096 \to 128$) + GELU + Linear($128 \to 14336$), trained to produce per-neuron importance scores that minimize output-distribution divergence under sparsity.
9	
10	\paragraph{Training.}
11	All predictors are trained for 1000 steps on WikiText-103 with sequence length 2048 and batch size 4 on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPU (96\,GB), using AdamW~\cite{loshchilov2019adamw} with learning rate $1 \times 10^{-3}$, weight decay $1 \times 10^{-2}$, and cosine decay.
12	The KL predictor uses Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation~\cite{jang2017gumbel} with temperature $\tau=1.0$ annealed to $0.1$ over training.
13	
14	\paragraph{Evaluation.}
15	We report perplexity on WikiText-2~\cite{merity2017pointer} (seq\_len=2048) and downstream accuracy on ARC-Challenge (25-shot), WinoGrande (5-shot), MMLU (5-shot), GSM8K (8-shot), HellaSwag (10-shot), and PIQA (0-shot).
16	
17	\paragraph{Inference Protocol.}
18	At inference, the predictor produces per-neuron scores that are passed to TEAL's global top-$k$ allocation mechanism~\cite{liu2025teal}, which distributes the sparsity budget across layers non-uniformly.
19	We term this \textit{predictor-TEAL}: the predictor replaces TEAL's magnitude-based scoring while retaining its allocation strategy.
20	
21	\paragraph{Baselines.}
22	We compare against: (1) \textbf{Vanilla TEAL}~\cite{liu2025teal}, which uses activation magnitudes directly\footnote{TEAL is deterministic; single-run evaluation suffices. KL/BCE predictors require multi-seed evaluation due to random initialization.}; (2) \textbf{BCE predictor} (Deja
…[truncated 15608 chars]

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our primary evaluation uses WikiText-2 perplexity, and downstream benchmarks reveal that perplexity is a poor proxy for reasoning ability at moderate sparsity: 40\% sparsity achieves PPL 10.76 but MMLU already degrades to 35.5\% (vs.\ 40.7\% at 30\%); 30\% appears to be the lowest-degradation sparsity level for downstream fidelity.
4	Training requires 1000 steps on WikiText-103 ($\sim$45 minutes on RTX PRO 6000 for KL, $\sim$17 minutes for BCE), but a separate predictor must be trained for each sparsity target.
5	On Mistral-7B (3-seed), KL improves PPL ($-$17.3\%) and commonsense tasks (WG, HS, PIQA: all seeds win) but MMLU is tied (43.3\% vs.\ 43.5\%) and GSM8K reverses across all seeds (7.0--11.1\% vs.\ TEAL 15.9\%). ARC-c is seed-dependent (37.8--50.1\% vs.\ TEAL 41.1\%; $\sigma$=5.2pp vs.\ LLaMA-8B's 2.5pp), indicating reduced cross-architecture metric stability. Forward KL's mass-covering property may preserve output breadth at the expense of reasoning-critical pathways.
6	The 30\% sparsity analysis is validated at 7--8B scale; at 14B, KL's MMLU decline ($-$2.79pp uniform, $-$8.1pp global) and GSM8K reversal ($-$20pp uniform; 32.1$\pm$5.2\% vs.\ TEAL's 52.1\%, 3-seed) reflect task-and-scale-dependent effects (\S\ref{sec:cross_arch}; Appendix~\ref{app:14b}): at 8B all methods collapse to $<$9\% GSM8K, so this reversal emerges only with sufficient base capacity. KL's high cross-seed GSM8K variance ($\sigma$=5.2pp) suggests minor training perturbations disrupt reasoning-critical pathways that magnitude pruning preserves more robustly. Scaling to 70B remains untested.
7	Out-of-distribution evaluation on C4 (Appendix~\ref{app:ood}) shows the KL advantage transfers but with a wider gap: C4 PPL at 30\% is $1.74\times$ dense vs.\ $1.46\times$ on in-domain WikiText-2, which indicates that the predictor partially overfits to training-domain activation patterns. Downstream task performance on C4 shows all six benchmarks within 1.1pp of in-dom
…[truncated 1603 chars]

_source line 914 · 2026-05-20T15:26:04.653483+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Our experiments demonstrate that \emph{coupling alignment}---optimizing a composable, output-level quantity---is necessary for sparsity prediction quality, a principle well-established in end-to-end learning but previously unverified for activation sparsity.
5	A target-swap ablation confirms that KL-discovered mask patterns (46--68\% divergent from magnitude) drive inference-stage quality; gradient coupling is necessary for mask discovery but not at inference.
6	A predictor ($<$1\% of base) reduces TEAL's perplexity by 14.5\% at matched sparsity and renders compensation networks redundant.
7	However, PPL gains do not uniformly transfer: KL improves commonsense reasoning (4/4 tasks) but underperforms on knowledge-intensive benchmarks (MMLU, GSM8K); cross-architecture evaluation (Mistral-7B, 3-seed: PPL and commonsense gains generalize; MMLU tied, GSM8K reverses) and scale analysis (14B: reasoning declines despite PPL gains) reveal task- and scale-dependent effects.
8	Future work includes N:M structured sparsity and scaling to 70B+.
9

27	
28	The full KL-sparse pipeline adds 6.4\% overhead to dense inference (126.78 vs.\ 119.11\,ms/forward), of which the predictor's 32-layer forward pass contributes 3.17\,ms (2.7\%); the remainder arises from mask application and sparse FFN indexing.
29	Eliminating the compensation network saves an additional $\sim$2.7\% latency overhead and 67.2M parameters (the per-layer GEMV required for output rescaling); KL distillation thus provides both quality and efficiency advantages over BCE-based approaches.
30	The overhead is near-identical at 30\% and 50\% sparsity because the predictor runs the same computation regardless of target sparsity; only the top-$k$ threshold changes.
31	
32	Note that the current implementation uses unstructured element-wise masks, which do not translate to wall-clock speedup without sparse kernel support.
33	The pipeline adds $\sim$6.4\% overhead

_source line 917 · 2026-05-20T15:26:12.033191+00:00_

1	Learned sparsity predictors for SwiGLU (Swish-Gated Linear Unit) LLMs universally use binary cross-entropy (BCE), training each neuron independently, yet inference allocates budgets globally via top-$k$ ranking.
2	We train predictors with Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, coupling all mask decisions through the output distribution.
3	At 30\% sparsity on LLaMA-3.1-8B, the KL predictor ($<$1\% of base parameters) reduces perplexity by 14.5\% relative to TEAL at matched sparsity (19\% at reported 28.5\% vs.\ TEAL's 30\%), improving commonsense reasoning (ARC +4.4pp, HellaSwag +7.8pp) but underperforming on knowledge-intensive benchmarks (MMLU 40.7\% vs.\ TEAL 45.9\%, both substantially below the dense 65.3\%). Post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) becomes redundant in prediction quality.
4	KL-derived masks diverge 46--68\% from magnitude-based masks (depending on sparsity level); a target-swap ablation confirms that these mask targets, discovered through KL's coupled gradients, are the proximate quality driver when transferred to simpler objectives.
5	Per-layer coupling (MSE) degrades below training-free baselines ($1.9\times$ TEAL at 30\%; $9.6\times$ at 50\%), suggesting that \emph{alignment} with the output distribution---not coupling per se---matters more than gradient coupling alone.
6	Cross-architecture evaluation on Mistral-7B-v0.3 (3-seed) and Qwen-2.5-14B reveals consistent PPL improvements but scale-dependent downstream effects.
7	At 50\% sparsity the perplexity advantage widens, though downstream reasoning collapses to near-random regardless of method, revealing a capacity cliff.
8

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	SwiGLU feed-forward networks~\cite{shazeer2020glu} in modern LLMs exhibit high contextual sparsity: at any given token, a large fraction of FFN neurons contribute negligibly to the output.
4	SwiGLU's gated activation makes prediction challenging: importance depends on both gate and up-projection values, a target that magnitude-based methods handle suboptimally.
5	Learned sparsity predictors~\cite{liu2023dejavu,gautam2026fastforward} exploit this property by training small networks to anticipate which neurons are important, but they universally rely on binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, treating each neuron as an independent classification target.
6	Yet at inference, sparsity budgets are allocated globally: the TEAL (training-free activation sparsity;~\citealp{liu2025teal}) top-$k$ mechanism ranks all neurons across all layers and retains only those above a model-wide threshold.
7	This mismatch between per-neuron training and global inference allocation has not been formally analyzed.
8	While MaskLLM~\cite{fang2024maskllm} applies KL-based mask optimization, it targets static N:M weight sparsity; activation sparsity requires masks that generalize to unseen inputs, vary per token, and coordinate across layers through global allocation.
9	
10	Gradient analysis reveals a structural asymmetry: BCE's gradient $\partial \mathcal{L}_{\text{BCE}} / \partial s_j = \sigma(s_j) - m_j^*$ depends only on neuron $j$, while under KL divergence, the gradient couples each neuron to all others through the downstream Jacobian (\S\ref{sec:method:gradient}).
11	KL's coupled gradients discover neuron importance patterns that diverge 68\% from magnitude-based masks (per-layer Jaccard 32\%).
12	A target-swap ablation confirms that these discovered mask targets are the proximate quality driver at inference: BCE trained on KL-derived targets reproduces KL quality (max $\Delta$$\leq$0.79pp at 50\%, s42), while magnitude-trained BCE achieves 28.13$\pm$0.20. Gradient 
…[truncated 2130 chars]

优势未能转化为下游任务一致性提升、且缺乏实际推理加速，限制了论文的影响力。\n\n### 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 核心实验方法论合理，消融设计好，但多种子覆盖不一致、MSE baseline 单种子、消融仅在50%稀疏度 建议：对 Table 2 所有行提供完整 3-seed 统计；MSE baseline 至少补 2 个种子。\n\n**N2** [critical] PPL 改善与下游任务脱节——核心实用性质疑\n- 具体位置：Table 2 (30% sparsity rows), §4.2 para 2-3\n- 问题：在 30%（唯一下游不崩溃的稀疏度），KL 的 PPL 比 TEAL 好 改善不能转化为下游提升甚至在关键任务上劣化，论文的实用贡献被严重削弱。\n- 建议：深入分析 MMLU/GSM8K 劣化的机制（per-task mask overlap 分析）；或在 claim 中显著降调。\n\n**N3** [critical] 消融实验仅在 50% 稀疏度——论文实际有用的 regime 未被消融覆盖\n- 具体位置：Table 4 (experiments.tex lines 127-152)\n- 问题：Table 4 所有消融仅在 50%。但论文自己承认 50% 时下游崩溃至随机水平（\"capacity-limited regime\"），30% 才是实际可用的稀疏度。如果 forward KL 在 30% 时相对 JSD/reverse KL 的优势缩小甚至消失，论文的 loss function 选择结论可能不成立。\n- 建议：在 30% 稀疏度补充 Forward KL / JSD / Reverse KL / STE 的消融比较。\n\n### Should-Fix 问题\n\n**W1** [warning] 稀疏度控制器在 30% 目标时失败（5% 偏差）——λ saturates at cap (5000), actual 28.5% vs target 30%。正文 headline claim \"19%\" 基于 mismatched 比较；matched-sparsity 比较（14.5%）应更显著。\n\n**W2** [warning] Forward KL 方向选择理论论证不足——标准 KD 文献通常论证 reverse KL 对 capacity-limited student 更优，与论文论点矛盾。实验验证了 forward > reverse，但理论解释需加强。\n\n**W3** [warning] 评估域同质性——训练 WikiText-103、评估 WikiText-2（同为 Wikipedia）。C4 OOD 显示 domain shift 放大 PPL（1.74× vs 1.44× dense）且跨种子方差增 30×（CV 6.3% vs 0.2%）。\n\n**W4** [warning] §3.6 梯度分析 informal——缺乏 KL 梯度的显式表达式、耦合程度量化、低 SNR 产生更好 mask 的正式解释。\n\n**W5** [warning] 无实际推理加速——当前实现增加 6.4% 开销。\n\n### 主要优势\n1. C1 目标-交换消融设计精妙，有效解耦 target quality 与 gradient coupling\n2. 诚实报告负面结果（MMLU/GSM8K 退化、Qwen-14B 反转、50% 下游崩溃）\n3. 全面的敏感性分析（bottleneck <3%、温度 <1.8%、训练步数 800 步饱和）\n4. §3.7 二维分类框架有用\n5. 单 GPU 45 分钟训练，高度可复现\n\n### 引用核查\n- verify_references: 47 entries, 0 orphan, 0 missing, 41 verified, 1 not_found (de2024griffin/Griffin), 5 minor mismatches (author name format/year)\n- 无虚假引用\n\n### 数据一致性核查\n- 表格间数字一致（Table 2 ↔ Table 5 ↔ Appendix tables 全部匹配）\n- Limitations 中 PPL 数值与正文表格匹配（40% PPL=10.76 ✓, MMLU=35.5% ✓, Mistral -17.3% ✓）\n- Abstract 中 \"ARC +4.4pp\"(43.7-39.33=4.37≈4.4 ✓), \"HellaSwag +7.8pp\"(71.07-63.31=7.76≈7.8 ✓)\n- 唯一轻微问题：Limitations 称 Mistral GSM8K \"7.0–11.1%\"，但 Table 17 仅报告 8.6±1.8，个别种子值未在表中展示\n\n### 格式合规\n- 正文 8 页（Section 1-5 在 page 1-8），合规\n- Double-blind：\\usepackage[review]{acl}，作者 \"Anonymous\"，无 GitHub 链接。GPU 型号（RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell）具体但不构成去匿名化\n- 引用格式符合 ACL natbib 规范\n\n**Confidence: 4/5**\n\n如果能补充 30% 消融和完整的 multi-seed Table 2，建议可升至 Conference（4）。",

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 **Reviewer 2** 的完整 Round 1 审稿意见。请逐条回应：对每个 Weakness/Question 标注 **[AGREE]** 并说明理由，或 **[REBUT]** 并引用论文具体 Section/Table/Figure 作为证据。

---

## Reviewer 2 完整审稿原文

### Summary
本文提出用 KL divergence 替代 BCE 训练 SwiGLU LLM 的上下文稀疏性预测器。核心思想是将 mask 决策通过完整前向传播耦合到输出分布，使每个 neuron 的梯度依赖于所有其他 neuron 的 mask 状态，而非独立优化。在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 上 50% 稀疏度下 PPL 相对 TEAL 降低 14.5%，并在 Mistral-7B 和 Qwen-2.5-14B 上做了跨架构验证。论文自述为"primarily empirical study"。

### Strengths
S1. 清晰的问题定义与动机。§3.6 的梯度分析简洁有力。
S2. 受控实验设计。消融实验设计合理。
S3. 丰富的附录支撑（10 页附录）。
S4. 训练效率高（1000 步，~45 min）。
S5. 负面结果的诚实报告。

### Weaknesses

**N1 [critical] 新颖性不足：核心贡献是标准 KL distillation 的直接应用。**
- KL divergence 匹配 teacher/student 输出分布是知识蒸馏经典范式。本文技术上未引入新 loss function、新优化方法或新理论分析。Gumbel-Sigmoid、adaptive Lagrangian、TEAL allocation 均为已有技术组合。
- §3.6 梯度分析本质是复述 "output-level loss 比 element-wise loss 传递更丰富梯度信号" 已知事实。
- 与 MaskLLM 区分：三个区分点存在，但核心训练原理高度重合。MaskLLM 用 Gumbel-Softmax + output loss，本文用 Gumbel-Sigmoid + KL loss，差异有限。

**N2 [critical] 缺少与已有训练型稀疏预测器的数值对比。**
- 主要对比 TEAL（training-free）和 magnitude-based pruning，但 §2 讨论的 DejaVu、ShadowLLM、FastForward 未在实验中数值对比。Taxonomy 预测力未被验证。

**W1 [warning] 无实际推理加速验证。** Appendix A 承认无 wall-clock speedup，增加 6.4% overhead。

**W2 [warning] 实验规模和多样性有限。** 主实验仅 LLaMA-3.1-8B；训练数据仅 WikiText-103；predictor 架构未与其他形式对比；缺少生成任务评估。

**W3 [warning] Table 1 taxonomy 分析价值有限。** 矩阵过于稀疏，预测力未验证，空 cell 未讨论。

**W4 [warning] C1 ablation 存在混淆因素。** Oracle masks 包含 KL 目标信息；更干净的 ablation 应用 frozen mask 直接评估。

### Minor Issues
M1. Forward vs reverse KL 选择应更充分论证。
M2. Griffin 引用关联较弱，speculation 无实验支撑。
M3. Figure 1 caption 不够自包含。
M4. CATS 引用可能需更新。
M5. Extended Training 叙述仓促。

### Questions
Q1. Table 3 ablation 是否做了多 seed？BCE+STE vs KL 差异 0.62 是否显著？
Q2. 为何不在 gate 分支做 sparsity prediction？
Q3. 是否尝试过 attention layer sparsity prediction？
Q4. Penalty controller 超参数敏感性？

### Missing References
MR1. Activation Sparsity Opportunities (arXiv:2412.12178)
MR2. 2:4 Activation Sparsity (arXiv:2503.16672)
MR3. Rethinking KL Divergence in KD (arXiv:2404.02657)

### Overall: 3.0 (Findings)

---

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
## Round 1 审稿任务 — Reviewer 1

### 你的侧重：方法严谨性 + 实验验证

重点审查以下方面：
1. **Claims vs Evidence 对齐**：论文声称的每个结论是否有充分的实验证据支持？特别关注 abstract 和 conclusion 中的 headline claims。
2. **实验设计**：baseline 选择是否公平？控制变量是否合理？是否遗漏了关键对比？
3. **统计严格性**：multi-seed 覆盖是否充分？哪些结果是 single-seed？标准差报告是否完整？
4. **消融完整性**：每个设计选择是否都有消融支持？是否存在未被消融覆盖的关键决策？
5. **数据核查**：表格中的数字是否自洽？正文引用与表格数据是否一致？
6. **方法严谨性**：Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation、adaptive penalty controller、KL 方向选择的论证是否严密？

### Venue 信息
- **EMNLP 2026**, ARR May cycle, deadline 2026-05-25
- Long Paper: 正文 ≤8 页, 参考文献无限制
- Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入页数
- ACL 模板, double-blind
- Overall Assessment: 5=Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle

### 论文路径
- LaTeX: `projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.tex`（含 abstract.tex, introduction.tex, method.tex, experiments.tex, related_work.tex, conclusion.tex, limitations.tex, ethics.tex, appendix.tex）
- PDF: `projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper/main.pdf`

### 论文摘要
论文提出用 KL divergence（而非 BCE）训练 SwiGLU LLM 的稀疏预测器。核心发现：KL 的耦合梯度发现与 magnitude-based masks 偏离 46-68% 的 mask patterns，这些 patterns 产生更低的 perplexity。Target-swap ablation (C1) 确认 mask quality 驱动推理阶段质量。评估在 LLaMA-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen-2.5-14B 上进行。

### 输出格式
请按以下结构输出审稿意见：
1. **Summary**（2-3 句）
2. **Strengths**（逐条编号，S1/S2/...）
3. **Weaknesses**（逐条编号，W1/W2/...，标注严重程度 [Critical/Major/Minor]）
4. **Questions**（逐条编号，Q1/Q2/...）
5. **Missing References**（如有）
6. **Overall Assessment**（评分 + 置信度 + 1-2 句总评）
